Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the algebras structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 726. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 504 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).
